Offer is for a 3% base price discount for a first-year Beazer employee or an internal Friends and Family participant. Buyer may choose to use the offer towards closing costs if the buyer completes the contribution requirements outlined in their contract. The closing costs offer is only valid if buyer completes a loan estimate comparison including at least two from Choice Lenders (&ldquo;Compare&rdquo;) and is only valid if buyer has completed the Compare and uses a Choice Lender to finance buyer&rsquo;s purchase of the home - see http://mortgagechoice.beazer.com for full program details. There is no affiliation or association between Beazer Homes and a Choice Lender. Each entity is independent and responsible for its own products, services and incentives. This offer is subject to underwriting guidelines which are subject to change without notice and which limit third-party contributions and may not be available on all loan products. Program and loan amount limitations apply. Not all buyers may qualify. Beazer Homes is not acting as a mortgage broker or lender. Buyers should consult with a mortgage broker or lender of their choice regarding mortgage loans and mortgage loan qualification. Any lender may be used, but failure to satisfy the Choice Contribution requirements forfeits the offer. There is no cash or exchange value for this credit offer and it may not be combined with any other offers or promotions. Options and savings vary depending on home, plan and community, and not all options are available in every home. Pricing, features and availability subject to change without notice. Prices may not include lot premiums, upgrades and/or options. Homes pictured may not be available/available at the lowest advertised price. Additional restrictions may apply. See New Home Counselor for complete details. &copy;2025 Beazer Homes

        function collapseSpecialOffers(event) {
            if (event.code == 'ArrowDown' || event.code == 'ArrowUp' || event.code == 'Space') {
                $('.specialOffers .collapsible-header').click();

The advertised price may not include lot premiums, upgrades and/or options. All home options are subject to availability and site conditions. Beazer reserves the right to change plans, specifications, and pricing without notice in its sole discretion. Square footages are approximate.  Exterior elevation finishes are subject to change without prior notice and may vary by plan and/or community. Interior design, features, decorator items, and landscape are not included. All renderings, color schemes, floor plans, maps, and displays are artists’ conceptions and are not intended to be an actual depiction of the home or its surroundings. A home’s purchase agreement will contain additional information, features, disclosures, and disclaimers. Please see New Home Counselor for individual home pricing and complete details. No Security Provided: If gate(s) and gatehouse(s) are located in the Community, they are not designed or intended to serve as a security system. Seller makes no representation, express or implied, concerning the operation, use, hours, method of operation, maintenance or any other decisions concerning the gate(s) and gatehouse(s) or the safety and security of the Home and the Community in which it is located. Buyer acknowledges that any access gate(s) may be left open for extended periods of time for the convenience of Seller and Seller’s subcontractors during construction of the Home and other homes in the Community. Buyer is aware that gates may not be routinely left in a closed position until such time as most construction within the Community has been completed. Buyer acknowledges that crime exists in every neighborhood and that Seller and Seller’s agents have made no representations regarding crime or security, that Seller is not a provider of security and that if Buyer is concerned about crime or security, Buyer should consult a security expert.<br/><br/>The utility cost shown is based on a particular home plan within each community as designed (not as built), using RESNET-approved software, RESNET-determined inputs and certain assumed conditions. The actual as-built utility cost on any individual Beazer home will be calculated by a RESNET-certified independent energy evaluator based on an on-site inspection and may vary from the as-designed rating shown on the advertisement depending on factors such as changes made to the applicable home plan, different appliances or features, and variation in the location and/or manner in which the home is built. Beazer does not warrant or guarantee any particular level of energy use costs or savings will be achieved. Actual energy utility costs will depend on numerous factors, including but not limited to personal utility usage, rates, fees and charges of local energy providers, individual home features, household size, and local climate conditions. The estimated utility cost shown is generated from RESNET-approved software using assumptions about annual energy use solely from the standard systems, appliances and features included with the relevant home plan, as well as average local energy utility rates available at the time the estimate is calculated. Where gas utilities are not available, energy utility costs in those areas will reflect only electrical utilities. Because numerous factors and inputs may affect monthly energy bill costs, buyers should not rely solely or substantially on the estimated monthly energy bill costs shown on this advertisement in making a decision to purchase any Beazer home. Beazer has no affiliation with RESNET or any other provider mentioned above, all of whom are third parties. <br /><br />  *When you shop and compare, you know you're getting the lowest rates and fees available. Lender competition leads to less money out of pocket at closing and lower payments every month. The Consumer Financial Protection Bureau (CFPB) found in their 2015 Consumer Mortgage Experience Survey that shopping for a mortgage saves consumers an average of .5% on their interest rate. Using this information, the difference between a 5% and a 4.5% interest rate on a new home that costs $315,000 (with a $15,000 down payment and a financed amount of $300,000) is a Principal & Interest savings of roughly $90 per month. Over a typical 30-year amortized mortgage, $90 per month adds up to $32,400 in savings over the life of the loan. To read more from the CFPB, please visit <a href="https://mortgagechoice.beazer.com/home/legalpage" target="_blank">https://mortgagechoice.beazer.com/<wbr />home/legalpage</a>.</div>
                        </div>
                    </div>
                </div>

            </div>

<script>

    // Called after request to email or text driving directions is submitted
    function requestDirectionsDesktopSuccess_a0954098_26a4_42f7_96c9_8f10347eaab4(response, status, jQXHR) {
        if (response.success) {
            var $formsPanel = $("#forms_panel_a0954098_26a4_42f7_96c9_8f10347eaab4");
            var contactinfo = Beazer.Cookies.GetContactInfo();
            if (response.via == "Text") {
                var $textMeContainer = $formsPanel.find(".visitus_textme_body");
                contactinfo.Phone = $textMeContainer.find("#phoneDesktop").val();
                $textMeContainer.find('form').addClass('hide');
                $textMeContainer.find('.success-message').removeClass('hide');

            } else {
                var $emailMeContainer = $formsPanel.find(".visitus_emailme_body");
                contactinfo.Email = $emailMeContainer.find("#emailDesktop").val();
                $emailMeContainer.find('form').addClass('hide');
                $emailMeContainer.find('.success-message').removeClass('hide');
            }
            Beazer.Cookies.SetContactInfo(contactinfo);
        } else Beazer.Dialogs.ShowDialog('Error', "<p class='padded-all no-margin'>" + response.error + "</p>");
    }

    function onLoad(wait) {
        if (document.readyState !== 'complete' || typeof (Beazer) == "undefined") return;

        if (wait) {
            clearInterval(wait);
        }

        var $mainPanel = $("#main_panel_a0954098_26a4_42f7_96c9_8f10347eaab4");
        var $formsPanel = $("#forms_panel_a0954098_26a4_42f7_96c9_8f10347eaab4");
        var $textMeContainer = $formsPanel.find(".visitus_textme_body");
        var $emailMeContainer = $formsPanel.find(".visitus_emailme_body");

        // Load cookies values into the dialogs
        var contactinfo = Beazer.Cookies.GetContactInfo();
        if (contactinfo != null) {
            $textMeContainer.find("#phoneDesktop").val(contactinfo.Phone);
            $emailMeContainer.find("#emailDesktop").val(contactinfo.Email);
        }

        // Initialize the forms
        $textMeContainer.beazerform();
        $emailMeContainer.beazerform();

        // wire panel toggle
        $mainPanel.on('click', 'a.panel-handler[data-target]', function(e) {
            $mainPanel.css('visibility', 'hidden');
            $formsPanel.removeClass('hide');
            if ($(this).data().target == 'textme') {
                $textMeContainer.removeClass('hide');
                $emailMeContainer.addClass('hide');
            }
            if ($(this).data().target == 'emailme') {
                $textMeContainer.addClass('hide');
                $emailMeContainer.removeClass('hide');
            }
            e.preventDefault();
        });
        $formsPanel.on('click', 'a.visitus_desktop_back_link', function(e) {
            $mainPanel.css('visibility', 'visible');
            $formsPanel.addClass('hide');
            e.preventDefault();
        });

        //Let's wire up an event to load the Text Me and Email me Form via ajax to support the anti-forgery token
        $('a[data-target ^="#VisitUsDesktopDirections"]').click(function() {

            var modelID = $(this).data("id");
            var primaryProductLineID = $(this).data("primaryproductlineid");
            var screensize = Beazer.Detect.Screen.IsLarge() ? "Desktop" : "Mobile";

            // Focus on the modal
            let modal = $('#VisitUsDesktopDirections_' + modelID + ' #main_panel_' + modelID + ' .visitus_desktop_directions_mapcontainer');

            //Text Me
            $.ajax({
                url: "/Contact/RequestDirectionsDeskTopTextForm",
                data: {
                    id: modelID,
                    primaryProductlineId: primaryProductLineID,
                    screenSize: screensize
                },
            }).done(function(response, status, jqXHR) {
                $('#TextMeFormSection_' + modelID).html(response);
                $('#TextMeFormSection_' + modelID).find('form').beazerform();
                focusVisitUsModal('#VisitUsDesktopDirections_' + modelID);
            });

            //Email Me
            $.ajax({
                url: "/Contact/RequestDirectionsDeskTopEmailForm",
                data: {
                    id: modelID,
                    primaryProductlineId: primaryProductLineID,
                    screenSize: screensize
                },
            }).done(function(response, status, jqXHR) {
                $('#EmailMeFormSection_' + modelID).html(response);
                $('#EmailMeFormSection_' + modelID).find('form').beazerform();
                focusVisitUsModal('#VisitUsDesktopDirections_' + modelID);
            });
        });

        $('.visitus_desktop_modal').on('keyup', function (event) {
            if (event.key == 'Escape') {
                $('.modal-handler.red-text.underlined[data-target="#' + $(this).attr('id') + '"]').focus();
            }
        });

        $('.visitus_desktop_modal .modal-close').on('keyup', function (event) {
            const key = event.key;
            if (key == 'Enter' || key == ' ' || key == 'Escape') {
                const id = $(this).attr('id').replace('close_','');
                $(this).click();
                $('.modal-handler.red-text.underlined[data-target="#VisitUsDesktopDirections_' + id + '"]').focus();
            }
        });
    }

    function focusVisitUsModal(selector) {
        setTimeout(function () {
            const modal = $(selector)[0]; 
            $(modal).attr('tabindex', 0);
            $(modal).find('.dialog_header').focus();
        }, 500);
    }

    const isDinamicallyInserted = 'False' === 'True';

    if (isDinamicallyInserted) {
        onLoad();
    } else {
        (function() {
            var wait = setInterval(function() {
                onLoad(wait);
            }, 500)
        })();
    }

</script>

<script>

    $(document).ready(function () {

        var isCommunityPage = false;

        //Update the URL with Query Params
        $('#ScheduleTour_SelectaNHC_Container_1673 a.scheduletour_nhc_button_1673').each(function () {

            let planName = $(this).parents('#ScheduleTour_CP_1673').data('planname');
            let homeSiteNumber = $(this).parents('#ScheduleTour_CP_1673').data('homesitenumber');
            let productlineids = $(this).parents('#ScheduleTour_CP_1673').data('productlineids');

            let url = new URL($(this).data('bookappturl'));
            let params = new URLSearchParams(url.search.slice(1));

            //Add the PLIDs
            if (productlineids != '') {
                params.append('bcom_product_line_id__c', productlineids);
            }
            //Add the LeadSourceID if it exists
            if (Beazer.Cookies.GetLeadSourceID() != 0) {
                params.append('leadsourceid__c', Beazer.Cookies.GetLeadSourceID());
            }
            else if (Beazer.Detect.Screen.IsMobile()) {
                params.append('leadsourceid__c', 1);
            }
            else {
                params.append('leadsourceid__c', 0);
            }
            //Add the GCLID if it exists
            if (Beazer.Cookies.GetGCLID() != 0) {
                params.append('bcom_gclid__c', Beazer.Cookies.GetGCLID());
            }
            //Add the Web Comments it if exists
            if ($('body').data('webcomments') != '') {
                params.append('web_comments__c', $('body').data('webcomments'));
            }
            //Add the plan name if it exists
            if (planName != '') {
                params.append('planofinterest__c', planName);
            }
            //Add the Lot Number if it exists
            if (homeSiteNumber != '') {
                params.append('specofinterest__c', homeSiteNumber);
            }
            //Update the NHCs book appt url
            $(this).attr('data-bookappturl', url + '?' + params);
        });

        function AddParameters(nobuttonurl) {
            let url = new URL(nobuttonurl.data('bookappturl'));
            let planName = nobuttonurl.parents('#ScheduleTour_CP_1673').data('planname');
            let homeSiteNumber = nobuttonurl.parents('#ScheduleTour_CP_1673').data('homesitenumber');
            let productlineids = nobuttonurl.parents('#ScheduleTour_CP_1673').data('productlineids');

            let params = new URLSearchParams(url.search.slice(1));
            //Add the PLIDs
            if (productlineids != '') {
                params.append('bcom_product_line_id__c', productlineids);
            }
            //Add the LeadSourceID if it exists
            if (Beazer.Cookies.GetLeadSourceID() != 0) {
                params.append('leadsourceid__c', Beazer.Cookies.GetLeadSourceID());
            }
            else if (Beazer.Detect.Screen.IsMobile()) {
                params.append('leadsourceid__c', 1);
            }
            else {
                params.append('leadsourceid__c', 0);
            }
            //Add the GCLID if it exists
            if (Beazer.Cookies.GetGCLID() != 0) {
                params.append('bcom_gclid__c', Beazer.Cookies.GetGCLID());
            }
            //Add the Web Comments it if exists
            if ($('body').data('webcomments') != '') {
                params.append('web_comments__c', $('body').data('webcomments'));
            }
            //Add the plan name if it exists
            if (planName != '') {
                params.append('planofinterest__c', planName);
            }
            //Add the Lot Number if it exists
            if (homeSiteNumber != '') {
                params.append('specofinterest__c', homeSiteNumber);
            }

            return '?' + params;
        }

        function ConfigureHaveYouVisitedButtons() {
            //Set the data dash for the Select a Series Container
            var selectaNHC = '#Scheduletour_NHCContainer_5002';
            $('#ScheduleTour_HaveyouVisited_Yes_1673').attr('data-nhccontainerid', selectaNHC);

            //Set the Gatherings Book Appointment for the PL Chosen on the NO button
            var plid = '5002';
            var params = AddParameters($('#SelectaSeriesButton_' + plid));
            $('#ScheduleTour_HaveyouVisited_No_1673').attr('data-bookappturl', $('#SelectaSeriesButton_' + plid).data('bookappturl') + params);

        }

        function ResetModal() {

            //Chili Piper Only SPL
            if (true && !true) {
                $('#ScheduleTour_SelectaTourType_Container_1673').hide();
                $('#ScheduleTour_SelectaSeries_Container_1673').hide();
                $('#ScheduleTour_SelectaNHC_Container_1673').hide();

                if (false) {
                    $('#ScheduleTour_HaveyouVisited_Container_1673').show();
                }

                ConfigureHaveYouVisitedButtons();
            }

            //Chili Piper Only MPL
            if (false && !true) {
                $('#ScheduleTour_SelectaTourType_Container_1673').hide();
                $('#ScheduleTour_HaveyouVisited_Container_1673').hide();
                $('#ScheduleTour_SelectaNHC_Container_1673').hide();

                //If we are on a Series,Plan,or Spec Page let's setup the Modal
                if (false) {

                    //Hide the Select a Series
                    $('#ScheduleTour_SelectaSeries_Container_1673').hide();
                    //Show the Have You Visited
                    $('#ScheduleTour_HaveyouVisited_Container_1673').show();
                    $('#ScheduleTour_CP_1673').scrollTop($(document).height());

                    ConfigureHaveYouVisitedButtons();
                }

            }

            //Chili Piper and Self-Guided Tour SPL
            if (true && true) {
                $('#ScheduleTour_SelectaSeries_Container_1673' ).hide();
                $('#ScheduleTour_HaveyouVisited_Container_1673').hide();
                $('#ScheduleTour_SelectaNHC_Container_1673').hide();

                //If a In Person or Virtual is Selected invert the color and show the Have you visited Section
                $('.selectaTourTypeButtonInPersonOrVirtual_1673').click(function () {
                    //reset the buttons
                    $('.selectatourtypebutton_1673').addClass('red-button');
                    $('.selectatourtypebutton_1673').removeClass('white-button');
                    //re-hide the select a series section
                    $('.selectaseriescontainer_1673').hide();

                    $(this).removeClass('red-button');
                    $(this).addClass('white-button');

                    ConfigureHaveYouVisitedButtons();

                    $('#ScheduleTour_HaveyouVisited_Container_1673').show();
                    $('#ScheduleTour_CP_1673').scrollTop($(document).height());
                });

            }

            //Chili Piper and Self-Guided Tour MPL
            if ((true && true) && (false || false)) {
                $('#ScheduleTour_SelectaSeries_Container_1673').hide();
                $('#ScheduleTour_HaveyouVisited_Container_1673').hide();
                $('#ScheduleTour_SelectaNHC_Container_1673').hide();
                var plid = '5002';

                //if it's Series Plan or Spec and there is no self-guided tour for the specific ProductLine
                if (false) {
                    //if there is no Self-guided tour for the particular Plan Spec set up the Modal
                    if ($('#ScheduleTourSelfGuidedTour_Productline_Container_5002').length === 0) {
                        $('#ScheduleTour_SelectaTourType_Container_1673').hide();
                        $('#ScheduleTour_HaveyouVisited_Container_1673').show();
                        $('#ScheduleTour_CP_1673').scrollTop($(document).height());

                        var selectaNHC = '#Scheduletour_NHCContainer_5002';
                        $('#ScheduleTour_HaveyouVisited_Yes_1673').attr('data-nhccontainerid', selectaNHC)

                        var params = AddParameters($('#SelectaSeriesButton_' + plid));
                        $('#ScheduleTour_HaveyouVisited_No_1673').attr('data-bookappturl', $('#SelectaSeriesButton_' + plid).data('bookappturl') + params);
                    }

                    //turn the Self-Guided Tour button to an external link
                    var link = $('#SelectaSeriesSelfGuidedTourButton_' + plid).attr('href');
                    $('#SelectaTourTypeButton_SelfGuidedTour_SeriesPlanSpec_1673').attr('href', link);
                }

                //If a In Person or Virtual is Selected invert the color and show the Have you visited Section
                $('.selectaTourTypeButtonInPersonOrVirtual_1673').click(function () {
                    var communityID = $(this).parents('#ScheduleTour_CP_1673').data('communityid');
                    var isSeriesPlanSpec = $(this).parents('#ScheduleTour_CP_1673').data('isseriesplanspec');
                    //console.log(communityID);
                    //reset the buttons
                    $('.selectatourtypebutton_1673').addClass('red-button');
                    $('.selectatourtypebutton_1673').removeClass('white-button');
                    //re-hide the select a series section
                    $('.selectaseriescontainer_1673').hide();

                    $(this).removeClass('red-button');
                    $(this).addClass('white-button');

                    //If we are on a Series,Plan,or Spec Page let's setup the Modal
                    if (isSeriesPlanSpec) {

                        //Show the Have You Visited
                        $('#ScheduleTour_HaveyouVisited_Container_' + communityID).show();
                        $('#ScheduleTour_CP_1673').scrollTop($(document).height());
                        //Set the data dash for the Select a Series Container
                        var selectaNHC = '#Scheduletour_NHCContainer_5002';
                        $('#ScheduleTour_HaveyouVisited_Yes_1673').attr('data-nhccontainerid', selectaNHC);

                        //Set the Gatherings Book Appointment for the PL Chosen on the NO button
                        var plid = '5002';
                        var params = AddParameters($('#SelectaSeriesButton_' + plid));;
                        $('#ScheduleTour_HaveyouVisited_No_1673').attr('data-bookappturl', $('#SelectaSeriesButton_' + plid).data('bookappturl') + params);
                    }
                    else {
                        var amountofSeries = $('#ScheduleTour_SelectaSeries_Container_' + communityID + ' .scheduletour_productline_container a').length
                        if (amountofSeries > 1) {
                            $('#ScheduleTour_SelectaSeries_Container_' + communityID).show();
                        }
                        else {
                            ConfigureHaveYouVisitedButtons();
                            $('#ScheduleTour_HaveyouVisited_Container_' + communityID).show();
                        }
                        $('#ScheduleTour_CP_1673').scrollTop($(document).height());
                    }


                });

                //If a Self-Guided Tour is Selected invert the color and show the Series that have a UtourURL and open the links in a new tab
                $('.selectaTourTypeButtonSelfGuidedTour_1673').click(function () {
                    var communityID = $(this).parents('#ScheduleTour_CP_1673').data('communityid');
                    var isSeriesPlanSpec = $(this).parents('#ScheduleTour_CP_1673').data('isseriesplanspec');

                    //reset the buttons
                    $('.selectatourtypebutton_1673').addClass('red-button');
                    $('.selectatourtypebutton_1673').removeClass('white-button');
                    //re-hide the select a series section
                    $('.selectaseriescontainer_1673').hide();

                    $(this).removeClass('red-button');
                    $(this).addClass('white-button');

                    //If we are on a Series,Plan,or Spec Page let's setup the Modal
                    if (isSeriesPlanSpec) {
                        $('#ScheduleTourSelfGuidedTour_SelectaSeries_Container_' + communityID).show();
                        $('#ScheduleTour_CP_1673').scrollTop($(document).height());
                        $('#ScheduleTourSelfGuidedTour_SelectaSeries_Container_' + communityID + ' #ScheduleTourSelfGuidedTour_Productline_Container_5002').show();
                    }
                    else {
                        // the Series Selection for Self Guided Tour
                        $('#ScheduleTourSelfGuidedTour_SelectaSeries_Container_' + communityID).show();
                        $('#ScheduleTour_CP_1673').scrollTop($(document).height());
                        $('#ScheduleTourSelfGuidedTour_SelectaSeries_Container_' + communityID + ' .scheduletourselfguidedtour_productline_container').show();
                    }

                });
            }

            //Self-Guided Tour Only MPL
            if (true && !true) {
                $('#ScheduleTour_SelectaTourType_Container_1673').hide();
                $('#ScheduleTour_HaveyouVisited_Container_1673').hide();
                $('#ScheduleTour_SelectaNHC_Container_1673').hide();

            }
        }

        ResetModal();

        //If a Series is Selected invert the color and show the Have you visited Section
        $('.selectaseriesbutton_1673').click(function () {
            var communityID = $(this).parents('#ScheduleTour_CP_1673').data('communityid');
            var hasChiliPiper = $(this).parents('#ScheduleTour_CP_1673').data('haschilipiper');

            //reset all the series buttons to red
            $('.selectaseriesbutton_1673').addClass('red-button');
            $('.selectaseriesbutton_1673').removeClass('white-button');
            //reset the Have you Visited Section
            $('#ScheduleTour_HaveyouVisited_Container_' + communityID).hide();
            //reset the Yes/NO buttons to red
            $('.haveyouvisitedbutton_1673').addClass('red-button');
            $('.haveyouvisitedbutton_1673').removeClass('white-button');
            $('#ScheduleTour_SelectaNHC_Container_' + communityID).hide();
            //reset the NHC buttons to red
            $('.scheduletour_nhc_button_1673').addClass('red-button');
            $('.scheduletour_nhc_button_1673').removeClass('white-button');
            $('#ScheduleTour_SelectaNHC_Container_' + communityID).hide();
            $('.selectatournhccontainer_1673').hide();
            $('#ScheduleTour_CP_1673').scrollTop($(document).height());

            $(this).removeClass('red-button');
            $(this).addClass('white-button');
            //Since a Series button was clicked we are on a Community Page
            isCommunityPage = true;
            //set the Gatherings Book Appointment for the PL Chosen on the NO button
            $(this).parents('#ScheduleTour_CP_1673').data('productlineids', $(this).attr('data-productlineid'));
            var params = AddParameters($(this));
            $('#ScheduleTour_HaveyouVisited_No_1673').attr('data-bookappturl', $(this).data('bookappturl') + params);

            if (hasChiliPiper) {
                $('#ScheduleTour_HaveyouVisited_Container_' + communityID).show();
                $('#ScheduleTour_CP_1673').scrollTop($(document).height());
            }

            var selectaNHC = $(this).data('nhccontainerid');
            $('#ScheduleTour_HaveyouVisited_Yes_1673').attr('data-nhccontainerid', '#' + selectaNHC);
        });
        //If Yes is clicked invert the color of the button and show the NHC Selection
        $('#ScheduleTour_HaveyouVisited_Yes_1673').click(function () {
            var communityID = $(this).parents('#ScheduleTour_CP_1673').data('communityid');

            //reset the Yes/NO buttons to red
            $('.haveyouvisitedbutton_1673').addClass('red-button');
            $('.haveyouvisitedbutton_1673').removeClass('white-button');

            $(this).removeClass('red-button');
            $(this).addClass('white-button');

            var selectaNHCID = $('#ScheduleTour_HaveyouVisited_Yes_1673').attr('data-nhccontainerid');
            $('#ScheduleTour_SelectaNHC_Container_' + communityID).show();
            $(selectaNHCID).show();
            $('#ScheduleTour_CP_1673').scrollTop($(document).height());
        });
        //If No is clicked open the Chili Piper iframe Community Tour Selection
        $('#ScheduleTour_HaveyouVisited_No_1673').click(function () {

            //reset the Yes/NO buttons to red
            $('.haveyouvisitedbutton_1673').addClass('red-button');
            $('.haveyouvisitedbutton_1673').removeClass('white-button');

            $(this).removeClass('red-button');
            $(this).addClass('white-button');

            $('#mainpanel_1673').addClass('hide');
            $('#ChiliPiperiframePanel_1673 iframe').prop('src', $(this).data('bookappturl'));
            $('#ChiliPiperiframePanel_1673').removeClass('hide');
            document.getElementById("BackToSelectionsLink_1673").scrollIntoView();
            document.getElementById("#ScheduleTour_CP_1673").scrollIntoView();
            SetiFrameWidth();
        });
        //If the NHC Buttons are clicked update the iframe src
        $('.scheduletour_nhc_button_1673').click(function () {
            let productlineids = $(this).parents('#ScheduleTour_CP_1673').data('productlineids');

            //reset the NHC buttons to red
            $('.scheduletour_nhc_button_1673').addClass('red-button');
            $('.scheduletour_nhc_button_1673').removeClass('white-button');

            $(this).removeClass('red-button');
            $(this).addClass('white-button');

            if (isCommunityPage) {
                $('#ChiliPiperiframePanel_1673 iframe').attr('src', $(this).attr('data-bookappturl') + '&bcom_product_line_id__c=' + productlineids);
            }
            else {
                $('#ChiliPiperiframePanel_1673 iframe').attr('src', $(this).attr('data-bookappturl'));
            }

            $('#mainpanel_1673').addClass('hide');
            $('#ChiliPiperiframePanel_1673').removeClass('hide');
            document.getElementById("BackToSelectionsLink_1673").scrollIntoView();
            document.getElementById("ScheduleTour_CP_1673").scrollIntoView();
            SetiFrameWidth();
        });

        function SetiFrameWidth() {
            $('#ScheduleTour_CP_1673').removeClass('scheduletour_cp_modal');
        }

        //Once in the iframe we need a way to return back to Selections
        $('#BackToSelectionsLink_1673').click(function () {

            //Let's reset all the buttons back to red and re-set the modal
            $('#mainpanel_1673 a').removeClass('white-button');
            $('#mainpanel_1673 a').addClass('red-button');
            ResetModal();

            $('#mainpanel_1673').removeClass('hide');
            $('#ChiliPiperiframePanel_1673').addClass('hide');
            $('#ScheduleTour_CP_1673').addClass('scheduletour_cp_modal');
        });
    });

</script>

    <script>

        // Called after request to email or text driving directions is submitted
        function requestDirectionsSuccess_a0954098_26a4_42f7_96c9_8f10347eaab4(response, status, jQXHR) {
            if (response.success) {
                var contactinfo = Beazer.Cookies.GetContactInfo();
                if (response.via == "Text") {
                    var $dialog = $("#VisitUsTextMeDialog_a0954098_26a4_42f7_96c9_8f10347eaab4.open");
                    contactinfo.Phone = $dialog.find("#phone").val();
                    Beazer.Dialogs.ShowDialog("Success", "<p class='padded-all no-margin'>You should receive a text message shortly.</p>", null, function () {
                        $dialog.modal('close');
                    });
                }
                else {
                    var $dialog = $("#VisitUsEmailMeDialog_a0954098_26a4_42f7_96c9_8f10347eaab4.open");
                    contactinfo.Email = $dialog.find("#phone").val();
                    Beazer.Dialogs.ShowDialog("Success", "<p class='padded-all no-margin'>You should receive an email shortly.</p>", null, function () {
                        $dialog.modal('close');
                    });
                }
                Beazer.Cookies.SetContactInfo(contactinfo);
            }
            else Beazer.Dialogs.ShowDialog('Error', "<p class='padded-all no-margin'>" + response.error + "</p>");
        }

        // Wait for the document to be ready, then initilialize the EmailMe and TextMe forms.
        // Also, if not on iOS, hide the Apple Maps link
        (function () {
            var wait = setInterval(function () {
                if (document.readyState !== 'complete') return;
                clearInterval(wait);

                // Get the dialogs
                var $text = $("#VisitUsTextMeDialog_a0954098_26a4_42f7_96c9_8f10347eaab4");
                var $email = $("#VisitUsEmailMeDialog_a0954098_26a4_42f7_96c9_8f10347eaab4");
                var $directions = $("#VisitUsDirectionsDialog_a0954098_26a4_42f7_96c9_8f10347eaab4");

                // Load cookies values into the dialogs
                var contactinfo = Beazer.Cookies.GetContactInfo();
                if (contactinfo != null) {
                    $text.find("#phone").val(contactinfo.Phone);
                    $email.find("#email").val(contactinfo.Email);
                }

                // Populate the user's screen size
                $(".visitus_screensize_input").val(Beazer.Detect.Screen.IsLarge() ? "Desktop" : "Mobile");

                // If not on iOS, hide the Apple Maps link
                if (Beazer.Detect.OS.IsiOS() == false) $directions.find(".visitus_links_openapplemaps").hide();

                // Initialize the forms
                $text.beazerform();
                $email.beazerform();

                //Let's wire up an event to load the Text Me and Email me Form via ajax to support the anti-forgery token
                $('a[data-target ^="#VisitUsTextMeDialog"]').click(function () {

                    var modelID = $(this).data("id");
                    var primaryProductLineID = $(this).data("primaryproductlineid");
                    var screenSize = Beazer.Detect.Screen.IsLarge() ? "Desktop" : "Mobile";


                    //Text Me
                    $.ajax({
                        url: "/Contact/RequestDirectionsTextForm",
                        data: { id: modelID, primaryProductlineId: primaryProductLineID, screenSize: screenSize },
                    }).done(function (response, status, jqXHR) {
                        $('#RequestDirectionsTextFormSection_' + modelID).html(response);
                        $('#RequestDirectionsTextFormSection_' + modelID).find('form').beazerform();
                    });

                });

                //Let's wire up an event to load the Text Me and Email me Form via ajax to support the anti-forgery token
                $('a[data-target ^="#VisitUsEmailMeDialog"]').click(function () {

                    var modelID = $(this).data("id");
                    var primaryProductLineID = $(this).data("primaryproductlineid");
                    var screenSize = Beazer.Detect.Screen.IsLarge() ? "Desktop" : "Mobile";

                    //Email Me
                    $.ajax({
                        url: "/Contact/RequestDirectionsEmailForm",
                        data: { id: modelID, primaryProductlineId: primaryProductLineID, screenSize: screenSize },
                    }).done(function (response, status, jqXHR) {
                        $('#RequestDirectionsEmailFormSection_' + modelID).html(response);
                        $('#RequestDirectionsEmailFormSection_' + modelID).find('form').beazerform();
                    });


                });

            }, 500)

            //Remove the Visit Us Slildeup if we are not on the Metro Page
            if (window.location.href.indexOf("search-") < 0) {
                $('.mobilemetrovisitusslideup').remove();
            }

        })();
    </script>

<script>

    $(document).ready(function () {

        //Parse the Hidden Model data to booleans
        var communityID = $('#CommunityID').val();
        var hasSelfGuidedTour = $('#HasSelfGuidedTour').val() === "True";
        var hasChiliPiper = $('#HasChiliPiper').val() === "True";
        var hasSelfGuidedTour = $('#HasSelfGuidedTour').val() === "True";
        var hasOneChiliPiper = $('#HasOneChiliPiper').val() === "True";
        var hasMultipleChiliPiper = $('#HasMultipleChiliPiper').val() === "True";
        var hasOneSelfGuidedTour = $('#HasOneSelfGuidedTour').val() === "True";
        var hasMultipleSelfGuidedTour = $('#HasMultipleSelfGuidedTour').val() === "True";
        var planName = $('#PlanName').val();
        var homeSiteNumber = $('#HomeSiteNumber').val();
        var productlineids = $('#ProductLineIDs').val();
        var isSeriesPlanSpec = $('#IsSeriesPlanSpec').val() === "True";
        var communityID = $('#CommunityID').val();
        var isCommunityPage = false;

        //Update the URL with Query Params
        $('.scheduletour_nhc_button').each(function () {

            let url = new URL($(this).data('bookappturl'));
            let params = new URLSearchParams(url.search.slice(1));

            //Add the PLIDs
            if (productlineids != '') {
                params.append('bcom_product_line_id__c', productlineids);
            }
            //Add the LeadSourceID if it exists
            if (Beazer.Cookies.GetLeadSourceID() != 0) {
                params.append('leadsourceid__c', Beazer.Cookies.GetLeadSourceID());
            }
            else if (Beazer.Detect.Screen.IsMobile()) {
                params.append('leadsourceid__c', 1);
            }
            else {
                params.append('leadsourceid__c', 0);
            }
            //Add the GCLID if it exists
            if (Beazer.Cookies.GetGCLID() != 0) {
                params.append('bcom_gclid__c', Beazer.Cookies.GetGCLID());
            }
            //Add the Web Comments it if exists
            if ($('body').data('webcomments') != '') {
                params.append('web_comments__c', $('body').data('webcomments'));
            }
            //Add the plan name if it exists
            if (planName != '') {
                params.append('planofinterest__c', planName);
            }
            //Add the Lot Number if it exists
            if (homeSiteNumber != '') {
                params.append('specofinterest__c', homeSiteNumber);
            }
            //Update the NHCs book appt url
            $(this).attr('data-bookappturl', url + '?' + params);
        });

        function AddParameters(nobuttonurl) {
            let url = new URL(nobuttonurl.data('bookappturl'));
            let params = new URLSearchParams(url.search.slice(1));
            //Add the PLIDs
            if (productlineids != '') {
                params.append('bcom_product_line_id__c', productlineids);
            }
            //Add the LeadSourceID if it exists
            if (Beazer.Cookies.GetLeadSourceID() != 0) {
                params.append('leadsourceid__c', Beazer.Cookies.GetLeadSourceID());
            }
            else if (Beazer.Detect.Screen.IsMobile()) {
                params.append('leadsourceid__c', 1);
            }
            else {
                params.append('leadsourceid__c', 0);
            }
            //Add the GCLID if it exists
            if (Beazer.Cookies.GetGCLID() != 0) {
                params.append('bcom_gclid__c', Beazer.Cookies.GetGCLID());
            }
            //Add the Web Comments it if exists
            if ($('body').data('webcomments') != '') {
                params.append('web_comments__c', $('body').data('webcomments'));
            }
            //Add the plan name if it exists
            if (planName != '') {
                params.append('planofinterest__c', planName);
            }
            //Add the Lot Number if it exists
            if (homeSiteNumber != '') {
                params.append('specofinterest__c', homeSiteNumber);
            }

            return '?' + params;
        }

        function ConfigureHaveYouVisitedButtons() {
            //Set the data dash for the Select a Series Container
            var selectaNHC = '#Scheduletour_NHCContainer_' + $('#ProductLineIDs').val();
            $('#ScheduleTour_HaveyouVisited_Yes').attr('data-nhccontainerid', selectaNHC);

            //Set the Gatherings Book Appointment for the PL Chosen on the NO button
            var plid = $('#ProductLineIDs').val()
            var params = AddParameters($('#SelectaSeriesButton_' + plid));
            $('#ScheduleTour_HaveyouVisited_No').attr('data-bookappturl', $('#SelectaSeriesButton_' + plid).data('bookappturl') + params);
            
        }

        function ResetModal() {

            //Chili Piper Only SPL
            if (hasOneChiliPiper && !hasSelfGuidedTour) {
                $('#ScheduleTour_SelectaTourType_Container_' + communityID).hide();
                $('#ScheduleTour_SelectaSeries_Container_' + communityID).hide();
                $('#ScheduleTour_SelectaNHC_Container_' + communityID).hide();

                if (isSeriesPlanSpec) {
                    $('#ScheduleTour_HaveyouVisited_Container_' + communityID).show();
                }

                ConfigureHaveYouVisitedButtons();
            }

            //Chili Piper Only MPL
            if (hasMultipleChiliPiper && !hasSelfGuidedTour) {
                $('#ScheduleTour_SelectaTourType_Container_' + communityID).hide();
                $('#ScheduleTour_HaveyouVisited_Container_' + communityID).hide();
                $('#ScheduleTour_SelectaNHC_Container_' + communityID).hide();

                //If we are on a Series,Plan,or Spec Page let's setup the Modal
                if (isSeriesPlanSpec) {

                    //Hide the Select a Series
                    $('#ScheduleTour_SelectaSeries_Container_' + communityID).hide();
                    //Show the Have You Visited
                    $('#ScheduleTour_HaveyouVisited_Container_' + communityID).show();
                    $('.scheduletour_cp_modal').scrollTop($(document).height());

                    ConfigureHaveYouVisitedButtons();
                }

            }

            //Chili Piper and Self-Guided Tour SPL
            if (hasOneChiliPiper && hasOneSelfGuidedTour) {
                $('#ScheduleTour_SelectaSeries_Container_' + communityID).hide();
                $('#ScheduleTour_HaveyouVisited_Container_' + communityID).hide();
                $('#ScheduleTour_SelectaNHC_Container_' + communityID).hide();

                //If a In Person or Virtual is Selected invert the color and show the Have you visited Section
                $('#SelectaTourTypeButton_InPersonOrVirtual').click(function () {
                    //reset the buttons
                    $('.selectatourtypebutton').addClass('red-button');
                    $('.selectatourtypebutton').removeClass('white-button');
                    //re-hide the select a series section
                    $('.selectaseriescontainer').hide();

                    $(this).removeClass('red-button');
                    $(this).addClass('white-button');

                    ConfigureHaveYouVisitedButtons();

                    $('#ScheduleTour_HaveyouVisited_Container_' + communityID).show();
                    $('.scheduletour_cp_modal').scrollTop($(document).height());
                });

            }

            //Chili Piper and Self-Guided Tour MPL
            if ((hasChiliPiper && hasSelfGuidedTour) && (hasMultipleChiliPiper || hasMultipleSelfGuidedTour)) {
                $('#ScheduleTour_SelectaSeries_Container_' + communityID).hide();
                $('#ScheduleTour_HaveyouVisited_Container_' + communityID).hide();
                $('#ScheduleTour_SelectaNHC_Container_' + communityID).hide();
                var plid = $('#ProductLineIDs').val()

                //if it's Series Plan or Spec and there is no self-guided tour for the specific ProductLine
                if (isSeriesPlanSpec) {
                    //if there is no Self-guided tour for the particular Plan Spec set up the Modal
                    if ($('#ScheduleTourSelfGuidedTour_Productline_Container_' + $('#ProductLineIDs').val()).length === 0) {
                        $('#ScheduleTour_SelectaTourType_Container_' + communityID).hide();
                        $('#ScheduleTour_HaveyouVisited_Container_' + communityID).show();
                        $('.scheduletour_cp_modal').scrollTop($(document).height());

                        var selectaNHC = '#Scheduletour_NHCContainer_' + $('#ProductLineIDs').val();
                        $('#ScheduleTour_HaveyouVisited_Yes').attr('data-nhccontainerid', selectaNHC)

                        var params = AddParameters($('#SelectaSeriesButton_' + plid));
                        $('#ScheduleTour_HaveyouVisited_No').attr('data-bookappturl', $('#SelectaSeriesButton_' + plid).data('bookappturl') + params);
                    }

                    //turn the Self-Guided Tour button to an external link
                    var link = $('#SelectaSeriesSelfGuidedTourButton_' + plid).attr('href');
                    $('#SelectaTourTypeButton_SelfGuidedTour_SeriesPlanSpec').attr('href', link);
                }

                //If a In Person or Virtual is Selected invert the color and show the Have you visited Section
                $('#SelectaTourTypeButton_InPersonOrVirtual').click(function () {

                    //reset the buttons
                    $('.selectatourtypebutton').addClass('red-button');
                    $('.selectatourtypebutton').removeClass('white-button');
                    //re-hide the select a series section
                    $('.selectaseriescontainer').hide();

                    $(this).removeClass('red-button');
                    $(this).addClass('white-button');

                    //If we are on a Series,Plan,or Spec Page let's setup the Modal
                    if (isSeriesPlanSpec) {

                        //Show the Have You Visited
                        $('#ScheduleTour_HaveyouVisited_Container_' + communityID).show();
                        $('.scheduletour_cp_modal').scrollTop($(document).height());
                        //Set the data dash for the Select a Series Container
                        var selectaNHC = '#Scheduletour_NHCContainer_' + $('#ProductLineIDs').val();
                        $('#ScheduleTour_HaveyouVisited_Yes').attr('data-nhccontainerid', selectaNHC);

                        //Set the Gatherings Book Appointment for the PL Chosen on the NO button
                        var plid = $('#ProductLineIDs').val();
                        var params = AddParameters($('#SelectaSeriesButton_' + plid));;
                        $('#ScheduleTour_HaveyouVisited_No').attr('data-bookappturl', $('#SelectaSeriesButton_' + plid).data('bookappturl') + params);
                    }
                    else {
                        var amountofSeries = $('#ScheduleTour_SelectaSeries_Container_' + communityID + ' .scheduletour_productline_container a').length
                        if (amountofSeries > 1) {
                            $('#ScheduleTour_SelectaSeries_Container_' + communityID).show();
                        }
                        else {
                            ConfigureHaveYouVisitedButtons();
                            $('#ScheduleTour_HaveyouVisited_Container_' + communityID).show();
                        }
                        $('.scheduletour_cp_modal').scrollTop($(document).height());
                    }


                });

                //If a Self-Guided Tour is Selected invert the color and show the Series that have a UtourURL and open the links in a new tab
                $('#SelectaTourTypeButton_SelfGuidedTour').click(function () {

                    //reset the buttons
                    $('.selectatourtypebutton').addClass('red-button');
                    $('.selectatourtypebutton').removeClass('white-button');
                    //re-hide the select a series section
                    $('.selectaseriescontainer').hide();

                    $(this).removeClass('red-button');
                    $(this).addClass('white-button');

                    //If we are on a Series,Plan,or Spec Page let's setup the Modal
                    if (isSeriesPlanSpec) {
                        $('#ScheduleTourSelfGuidedTour_SelectaSeries_Container_' + communityID).show();
                        $('.scheduletour_cp_modal').scrollTop($(document).height());
                        $('#ScheduleTourSelfGuidedTour_SelectaSeries_Container_' + communityID + ' #ScheduleTourSelfGuidedTour_Productline_Container_' + $('#ProductLineIDs').val()).show();
                    }
                    else {
                        // the Series Selection for Self Guided Tour
                        $('#ScheduleTourSelfGuidedTour_SelectaSeries_Container_' + communityID).show();
                        $('.scheduletour_cp_modal').scrollTop($(document).height());
                        $('#ScheduleTourSelfGuidedTour_SelectaSeries_Container_' + communityID + ' .scheduletourselfguidedtour_productline_container').show();
                    }

                });
            }

            //Self-Guided Tour Only MPL
            if (hasSelfGuidedTour && !hasChiliPiper) {
                $('#ScheduleTour_SelectaTourType_Container_' + communityID).hide();
                $('#ScheduleTour_HaveyouVisited_Container_' + communityID).hide();
                $('#ScheduleTour_SelectaNHC_Container_' + communityID).hide();

            }
        }

        ResetModal();

        //If a Series is Selected invert the color and show the Have you visited Section
        $('.selectaseriesbutton').click(function () {

            //reset all the series buttons to red
            $('.selectaseriesbutton').addClass('red-button');
            $('.selectaseriesbutton').removeClass('white-button');
            //reset the Have you Visited Section
            $('#ScheduleTour_HaveyouVisited_Container_' + communityID).hide();
            //reset the Yes/NO buttons to red
            $('.haveyouvisitedbutton').addClass('red-button');
            $('.haveyouvisitedbutton').removeClass('white-button');
            $('#ScheduleTour_SelectaNHC_Container_' + communityID).hide();
            //reset the NHC buttons to red
            $('.scheduletour_nhc_button').addClass('red-button');
            $('.scheduletour_nhc_button').removeClass('white-button');
            $('#ScheduleTour_SelectaNHC_Container_' + communityID).hide();
            $('.selectatournhccontainer').hide();
            $('.scheduletour_cp_modal').scrollTop($(document).height());

            $(this).removeClass('red-button');
            $(this).addClass('white-button');
            //Since a Series button was clicked we are on a Community Page
            isCommunityPage = true;
            //set the Gatherings Book Appointment for the PL Chosen on the NO button
            productlineids = $(this).attr('data-productlineid');
            var params = AddParameters($(this));
            $('#ScheduleTour_HaveyouVisited_No').attr('data-bookappturl', $(this).data('bookappturl') + params);

            if (hasChiliPiper) {
                $('#ScheduleTour_HaveyouVisited_Container_' + communityID).show();
                $('.scheduletour_cp_modal').scrollTop($(document).height());
            }

            var selectaNHC = $(this).data('nhccontainerid');
            $('#ScheduleTour_HaveyouVisited_Yes').attr('data-nhccontainerid', '#' + selectaNHC);
        });
        //If Yes is clicked invert the color of the button and show the NHC Selection
        $('#ScheduleTour_HaveyouVisited_Yes').click(function () {

            //reset the Yes/NO buttons to red
            $('.haveyouvisitedbutton').addClass('red-button');
            $('.haveyouvisitedbutton').removeClass('white-button');

            $(this).removeClass('red-button');
            $(this).addClass('white-button');

            var selectaNHCID = $('#ScheduleTour_HaveyouVisited_Yes').attr('data-nhccontainerid');
            $('#ScheduleTour_SelectaNHC_Container_' + communityID).show();
            $(selectaNHCID).show();
            $('.scheduletour_cp_modal').scrollTop($(document).height());
        });
        //If No is clicked open the Chili Piper iframe Community Tour Selection
        $('#ScheduleTour_HaveyouVisited_No').click(function () {

            //reset the Yes/NO buttons to red
            $('.haveyouvisitedbutton').addClass('red-button');
            $('.haveyouvisitedbutton').removeClass('white-button');

            $(this).removeClass('red-button');
            $(this).addClass('white-button');

            $('#mainpanel').addClass('hide');
            $('#ChiliPiperiframePanel iframe').prop('src', $(this).data('bookappturl'));
            $('#ChiliPiperiframePanel').removeClass('hide');
            document.getElementById("BackToSelectionsLink").scrollIntoView();
            document.getElementById("ScheduleTour_CP_ModalHeader").scrollIntoView();
            SetiFrameWidth();
        });
        //If the NHC Buttons are clicked update the iframe src
        $('.scheduletour_nhc_button').click(function () {

            //reset the NHC buttons to red
            $('.scheduletour_nhc_button').addClass('red-button');
            $('.scheduletour_nhc_button').removeClass('white-button');

            $(this).removeClass('red-button');
            $(this).addClass('white-button');

            if (isCommunityPage) {
                $('#ChiliPiperiframePanel iframe').attr('src', $(this).attr('data-bookappturl') + '&bcom_product_line_id__c=' + productlineids);
            }
            else {
                $('#ChiliPiperiframePanel iframe').attr('src', $(this).attr('data-bookappturl'));
            }
            
            $('#mainpanel').addClass('hide');
            $('#ChiliPiperiframePanel').removeClass('hide');
            document.getElementById("BackToSelectionsLink").scrollIntoView();
            document.getElementById("ScheduleTour_CP_ModalHeader").scrollIntoView();
            SetiFrameWidth();
        });

        function SetiFrameWidth() {
            $('#ScheduleTour_CP_' + communityID).removeClass('scheduletour_cp_modal');
        }

        //Once in the iframe we need a way to return back to Selections
        $('#BackToSelectionsLink').click(function () {

            //Let's reset all the buttons back to red and re-set the modal
            $('#mainpanel a').removeClass('white-button');
            $('#mainpanel a').addClass('red-button');
            ResetModal();

            $('#mainpanel').removeClass('hide');
            $('#ChiliPiperiframePanel').addClass('hide');
            $('#ScheduleTour_CP_' + communityID).addClass('scheduletour_cp_modal');
        });

        $('.chilipiperiframe').on('keyup blur', function (event) {
            $('#ScheduleTour_CP_ModalHeader').focus();
        });

        // Keyboard event to close Schedule Tour modal
        $('#ScheduleTour_CP_ModalHeader .modal-close').on('keyup', function (event) {
            const keycode = event.originalEvent.code;
            if (keycode == 'Space' || keycode == 'Enter')
                $(this).click();
        });

        // Keyboard event to click Yes/No Schedule Tour buttons
        $('.scheduletour_productline_container .haveyouvisitedbutton').on('keyup', function (event) {
            const keycode = event.originalEvent.code;
            if (keycode == 'Space' || keycode == 'Enter')
                $(this).click();
        });

        // Keyboard event to trigger InPersonOrVirtual button click
        $('#SelectaTourTypeButton_InPersonOrVirtual').on('keydown', function (event) {
            const keycode = event.originalEvent.code;
            if (keycode == 'Space' || keycode == 'Enter') {
                $(this).click();
            }
        });

        // Keyboard event to trigger SeflGuidedTour Spec button click
        $('#SelectaTourTypeButton_SelfGuidedTour_Spec').on('keydown', function (event) {
            const keycode = event.originalEvent.code;
            if (keycode == 'Space' || keycode == 'Enter') {
                $(this).click();
            }
        });

        // Keyboard event to trigger SeflGuidedTour SeriesPlanSpec button click
        $('#SelectaTourTypeButton_SelfGuidedTour_SeriesPlanSpec').on('keydown', function (event) {
            const keycode = event.originalEvent.code;
            if (keycode == 'Space' || keycode == 'Enter') {
                $(this).click();
            }
        });

        // Keyboard event to trigger SeflGuidedTour button click
        $('#SelectaTourTypeButton_SelfGuidedTour').on('keydown', function (event) {
            const keycode = event.originalEvent.code;
            if (keycode == 'Space' || keycode == 'Enter') {
                $(this).click();
            }
        });

        // Keyboard event to trigger Schedule Tour NHC button
        $('.scheduletour_nhc_button').on('keydown', function (event) {
            const keycode = event.originalEvent.code;
            if (keycode == 'Space' || keycode == 'Enter') {
                $(this).click();
            }
        });
    });

    // Keyboard event to trigger BackToSelectionsLink clik
    function triggerBackToSelectionsLink(event) {
        if (event.code == 'Enter' || event.code == 'Space') {
            $('#BackToSelectionsLink').click();
        }
    }

    function goToTopModal(event) {
        if (event.code == 'Tab') {
            $('#ScheduleTour_CP_ModalHeader .dialog_header').focus();
        }
    }

</script>

<footer class=toolbarvisible>

    <script type="text/javascript">

        // NOTE: This needs to run inline to ensure the share button is configured prior to its content being written to the screen.
        var addthis_ui_lang = "en";

        // Get the social page tracker API
        var socialPageTracker = null;
        try {
            socialPageTracker = _gat._getTracker(gaAccountId);
        }
        catch (e) { }

        // Configure the share button from AddThis
        var addthis_config = {
            data_track_addressbar: false,
            pubid: "ra-4d839cfc47eb6bd4",
            ui_language: addthis_ui_lang,
            ui_header_background: "#cc2b2b",
            ui_header_color: "#ffffff",
            ui_click: true
        };

        if (socialPageTracker != null) addthis_config.data_ga_tracker = socialPageTracker;

        var addthis_share = {};

        //Configure the images to show up on the pinterest share screen
        var addthis_config = {
            image_include: "at_include",
            image_exclude: "at_exclude"
        }

        // If the browser is IE, change how the Pinterest share button works since the AddThis button doesn't support IE11 (because it uses pinmarklet.js from Pinterest)
        $(document).on("click", ".beazer_button_pinterest_share", function (e) {
            var desc = $("head meta[name='description']").attr("content");
            var image = $("head meta[property='og:image']:eq(0)").attr("content");
            if (image == null || image.trim().length == 0) {
                var dim = 0; img = null;
                $("img").each(function () {
                    var th = $(this).height();
                    var tw = $(this).width();
                    if ((th * tw) > dim) {
                        dim = (th * tw);
                        img = this;
                    }
                });
                if (img != null) image = $(img).attr("src");
                if (image == null || image.trim().length == 0) {
                    // Show really never get here, but just in case
                    Beazer.Dialogs.ShowDialog("Nothing to Share", "<div class='padded-all'>Sorry. There are no available images on the page to share.<div>", null, null, '400', null, "");
                    e.preventDefault();
                    return;
                }
            }
            setTimeout(function () {
                var w = window.open('//www.pinterest.com/pin/create/button/?&url=' + encodeURI(document.location.href) + '&media=' + encodeURI(image) + '&description=' + encodeURI(desc), "pinterest_share", "width=600,height=700,resizable=yes");
                w.focus();
            }, 500);
            e.preventDefault();
        });


        $(document).ready(function () {
            let recizeNav = () => {
                if (screen.width >= 993) {
                    $("#hamburger").attr("tabindex", "-1");
                    $("#sideNav").attr("tabindex", "-1");
                    $("#sideNav").css("display", "none");

            addEventListener("resize", (event) => {
                recizeNav();
            });

            /* Code to detect changes in the browser zoom and to set the properties needed for the hamburger menu */
            const viewport = window.visualViewport;

            function zoomHandler() {
                let windowZoom = Math.round(window.devicePixelRatio * 100);

                if (windowZoom == 200) { 
                    $("#sideNav").css("display", "block");
                    $("#hamburger").attr("tabindex", "0");
                }

    <script>

        // Add a Recently Viewed Item for this Community
        Beazer.Cookies.AddRecentlyViewed({
            ID: 1673,
            SecondaryID: null,
            ItemType: 4,
            LastVisit: new Date()
        });

        var isViewInit = false;

        function initCommunityView() {

            var $communityHeader = $('.community_header');
            var $offers = $('.community_header').find('.specialOffers');
            var pushpinTop = $communityHeader.offset().top;

            // calculate scroll offset to take pushpin and offers into consideration (first scroll is off)
			var getScrollOffset = function() {
				if (Beazer.Detect.Device.IsMobile()) return 10;
                var scrollOffset = 200 + ($offers.length > 0 ? $offers.height() : 0);
                if ($(window).scrollTop() === 0) {
                    scrollOffset += $('.community_header').outerHeight();
                }
                return scrollOffset;
            }

            // scrollspy
            $('#reviewsAnchor, #visitAnchor').addClass('scrollspy'); // add class to sections in partials
            $('.scrollspy').scrollSpy({ getScrollOffset: getScrollOffset });

            // pushpin
            $communityHeader.pushpin({ top: pushpinTop });

            // scrollable anchors
            $('a.scrollable').scrollable({ getScrollOffset: getScrollOffset });

            // desktop VIP community intro layout
            if ($('.community_intro_overview_vip').length > 0) {
                $('.community_intro_overview').css('min-height', 'calc(100% - 200px)'); // don't shift down VIP banner
            }
            // resize community overview children to try to fit in container
            var $overview = $('.community_intro_overview');
            $overview._hasScrollBar = function() { return $overview[0].scrollHeight > $overview.innerHeight(); }
            $overview._compact = function() {
                if ($overview._hasScrollBar()) {
                    $overview.addClass('compact');
                    if ($overview._hasScrollBar()) {
                        $overview.addClass('extra');
                    }
                }
            }
            $overview._expand = function() {
                if (!$overview._hasScrollBar()) {
                    $overview.removeClass('extra');
                    if (!$overview._hasScrollBar()) {
                        $overview.removeClass('compact');
                    }
                }
            }
            $overview._resize = function() {
                if (Beazer.Detect.Screen.IsLarge()) {
                    $overview._expand();
                    $overview._compact();
                }
            }

            $(window).resize(function() {
                $overview._resize();
            });

            // ... initialize overview size when images are loaded
            function initOverviewSize() {
                if ($overview.height() > 0) {
                    $overview._resize();
                } else {
                    setTimeout(initOverviewSize, 400);
                }
            }

            initOverviewSize();
            // ...

			// init card lists originally hidden
			$('.community_index .card_list_outer.defer').removeClass('defer').beazerCardList();
            // init defer-tabs after each tab content is initialized while visible
            $('.community_index .defer-tabs:not(#areaAnchor .community_places)').addClass('tabs').tabs();
            // ... initialize local interest tabs after all maps are loaded
            var placeGroupsQty = $('#areaAnchor .community_places li.tab').length;
            var loadedQty = 0;
            $('#areaAnchor .card_list_outer').on('maploaded',
                function() {
                    loadedQty++;
                    if (loadedQty == placeGroupsQty) {
                        $('.community_index .defer-tabs.community_places').addClass('tabs').tabs();
                    }
                });

            // force tabs redraw (indicator)
            $('.community_places li a.active').click();

            var moreInfoButtonScrollPosition;
            initializeReviews();

            //Beazer Formify
            $('form').beazerform();

            //Manage focus; when the "More about this community" button becomes a "Less" button at
            // the bottom of the section, it drags focus down with it.
            $('#moreInfoButton').click(function() {
                if ($(this).attr('aria-expanded') == 'false') {
                    moreInfoButtonScrollPosition = $(window).scrollTop();
                    setTimeout(function() {
                            window.location.hash = "moreInfoTop";

                        },
                        250);
                } else {
                    if (moreInfoButtonScrollPosition) {

                        var body = $("html, body");
                        body.stop().animate({ scrollTop: moreInfoButtonScrollPosition },
                            250,
                            'swing',
                            function() {
                                moreInfoButtonScrollPosition = undefined;
                            });
                    }
                }
            });

            // community slider
            $('.community_intro .slider').beazerSlider();

            // community info, initialize more
            // ... places tabs initializes asynchronously, force indicators to redraw (workaround for iPhones)
            $('.community_info_container.bzh_more').beazerMore({
                onExpanded: function($moreHandler) {
                    $moreHandler.html($moreHandler.html().replace('More', 'Less').replace('big-down', 'big-up'));
                    $('.community_places li a.active').click();
                },
                onCollapsed: function($moreHandler) {
                    $moreHandler.html($moreHandler.html().replace('Less', 'More').replace('big-up', 'big-down'));
                }
            });

            // single product line tabs, initialize views
            $('.community_singleproductline_container .bzh_views').beazerViews();
            $(window).on('resize',
                function() {
                    // ... if transition from mobile to desktop, make sure cards sub-tab is visible (list tab is always invisible on desktop)
                    if (Beazer.Detect.Screen.IsLarge()) {
                        $('#homeDesigns').find('.bzh_views .card-view').parent().click();
                        $('#quickMoveIns').find('.bzh_views .card-view').parent().click();
                    }
                });

            // multiple product line tabs
            $(window).on('resize',
                function() {
                    // ... if transition from desktop to mobile, make sure series tab is visible (QMIs tab is always invisible on mobile)
                    if (Beazer.Detect.Screen.IsLarge() == false) {
                        $('#seriesAnchor').find('.tabs').tabs('select_tab', 'homeSeries');
                    }
                });

            // sort card lists
            $(document).on('change',
                '.bzh_views_view .select-wrapper.sort select',
                function() {
                    var $option = $(this).find("> option:selected");
                    var $lists = $(this).closest(".bzh_views").find(".card_list_outer");
                    if ($lists.length == 0) $lists = $(this).closest(".bzh_views_view").find(".card_list_outer");
                    $lists.beazerCardList('sort',
                        $option.attr("data-sortattribute"),
                        $option.attr("data-sortdirection"));
                });

            // KAMPYLE
            var KAMPYLE_FORM_ID = $('script#kampyle').data().formId;
            $('.kampyle').click(function() {
                KAMPYLE_ONSITE_SDK.showForm(KAMPYLE_FORM_ID);
                $('.kampyle.selected').removeClass('selected');
                $(this).addClass('selected');
                return false;
            });

            // gated content form
            $('.community_details_form .community_details_button').click(function() {
                var $this = $(this);
                var requestUrl = $this.attr('data-url');
                var communityId = $this.attr('data-community-id');
                var emailAddress = $this.closest('.community_details_form').find('input[name=email]').val()
                    .toLowerCase();
                var re =
                    /^(([^<>()\[\]\\.,;:\s@"]+(\.[^<>()\[\]\\.,;:\s@"]+)*)|(".+"))@((\[[0-9]{1,3}\.[0-9]{1,3}\.[0-9]{1,3}\.[0-9]{1,3}\])|(([a-zA-Z\-0-9]+\.)+[a-zA-Z]{2,}))$/;
                if (!re.test(emailAddress)) {
                    Beazer.Dialogs.ShowHelp('Community Details', 'Please enter a valid email.')
                    return
                }
                var screensize = Beazer.Detect.Screen.IsLarge() ? "Desktop" : "Mobile";
                $.ajax({
                    cache: false,
                    async: true,
                    type: "POST",
                    url: requestUrl,
                    data: { emailAddress: emailAddress, communityID: communityId, screensize: screensize },
                    dataType: 'json',
                    success: function(result) {
                        if (result != null && result.Success == true) {
                            $('.community_details_form').addClass('hide');
                            $('.community_details_thankyou').removeClass('hide');
                        } else {
                            alert(result.Error);
                        }
                    }
                });
            });

            // Send user to more reviews page
            $("#viewMoreReviews").on("click",
                function() {

                    Beazer.Cookies._SetCookieValue("SelectedReviewMetroID", 1025, false, 1);
                    Beazer.Cookies._SetCookieValue("SelectedReviewStateID",
                        1008,
                        false,
                        1);

                    window.location.href = "/reviews";
                });


            setupSpecialOffers();

            // Gatherings
            // ... Initialize homebuyer stories
            if ($('#homebuyer_story_slider').length > 0) {
                homebuyerStoryInit();
            }

            // Init community gatherings FAQs
            initCommunityGatheringsFAQs();

            isViewInit = true;
        }

        function initCommunityGatheringsFAQs() {
            // Swap icons on click
            $('ul#faqList li.expandable').find('a').click(function() {
                //Set the apporpiate icon on the currently clicked item
                var $currentIconSpan = $(this).find('span');
                if ($(this).hasClass('active')) {
                    // Show "hide" icon
                    $currentIconSpan.removeClass('collapse').addClass('expand');
                } else {
                    // Show "collapse" icon
                    $currentIconSpan.removeClass('expand').addClass('collapse');
                }
            });
        }

        var isQmisViewInit = false

        function initQmisView() {
			// init card lists originally hidden
			$('div.community_mobile_mpl_qmi .card_list_outer').removeClass('defer').beazerCardList();
            $('div.community_mobile_mpl_qmi .quickmoveins_views_container.bzh_views').beazerViews();
            // init defer-tabs after each tab content is initialized while visible
            $('div.community_mobile_mpl_qmi .defer-tabs').addClass('tabs').tabs();
            isQmisViewInit = true;

            if (Beazer.RouteHandler.Route.IsListView() && Beazer.Detect.Screen.IsLarge() == false) {
                if (Beazer.RouteHandler.Route.IsQMITab()) {
                    $('#quickMoveIns_tab').click();
                    $('#quickMoveIns').find('.list-view').click();
                    $('.quickmoveins_views_container').find('.list-view').click();
                } else {
                    $('span.list-view').click();
                }

                if ($('#viewAnchor').length > 0)
                    $('#viewAnchor')[0].scrollIntoView(true);
            }
        }

        $(document).ready(function () {

            
            Beazer.Forms.LoadCommunityLeadForm('.community_leadform_container', 1673, null, '', null);

            $('.community_details_container:not(.dim-none) .overlay').css('opacity', '0.8');

            //If it the /offer path open up the special offer header
            if (window.location.href.indexOf("offer") > -1) {
                $('.collapsible-header').click();
            }

            // swaping mobile qmis with desktop community
            if ($('#isMPL').val() == 'True' && Beazer.RouteHandler.Route.ShowQMIs()) {
                var ipadWidth = 0;
                $(window).on('resize',
                    function() {
                        clearTimeout(window.resizedFinishedQMI);
                        window.resizedFinishedQMI = setTimeout(function() {
                                // ... if transition from mobile to desktop, load Community page
                                if (Beazer.RouteHandler.Route.ShowQMIs()) {
                                    if (Beazer.Detect.Screen.IsLarge()) {
                                        $('div.community_mobile_mpl_qmi').hide();
                                        $('div.community_index').removeClass('hide').show();
                                        if (!isViewInit)
                                            initCommunityView();
                                        // if iPad, orientation changed, activate QMIs
                                        if (Beazer.Detect.Device.IsIPad()) {
                                            if (ipadWidth != $(window).width()) { // iOS triggers resize when scrolling
                                                // throttle transition for scrollspy to work properly
                                                $('.community_header_nav a.active').removeClass('active')
                                                $('a[href="#overviewAnchor"]').click();
                                                setTimeout(function() {
                                                        $('a[href="#seriesAnchor"][data-tab-id="quickMoveIns"]')
                                                            .click();
                                                    },
                                                    1000);
                                            }
                                        }
                                        // force tabs redraw (indicator)
                                        $('.tabs li.tab a.active').click();
                                    } else {
                                        setupSpecialOffers();
                                        $('div.community_mobile_mpl_qmi').removeClass('hide').show();
                                        if (!isQmisViewInit)
                                            initQmisView()
                                        $('div.community_index').hide();
                                    }
                                }
                                ipadWidth = $(window).width();
                            },
                            250);
                    });

                $(window).resize();
            } else {
                initCommunityView();
            }

            //inject OneLinkNoTx to the Multiple Product Lines section
            $('.community_multipleproductline_container h2:nth-child(2) span:nth-child(2)').addClass('OneLinkNoTx');

                    referenceId: referenceid,
                    onSuccess: function (data) {
                        if (data.presenceStatus == 'online') {

                            //display the chat icon
                            $('.desktop-chat').css('display', '');


                            //link the upper chat icon to the slide up window
                            $('.desktop-chat').click(function () {

                                //sdk call to start the chat
                                lpTag.sdk.exec("caoEngager.startChat", {
                                    referenceId: referenceid
                                });
                            }
                            );
                        }
                    },
                    onError: function (data) { console.log("An error has occured"); }
                });
                //END NEW CAO CODE */

            //add an aria-label “low maintenance living at {{Community Name}}”
            $('.gatherings_additionalvideo').attr('aria-label', 'secondary video for Emerson Estates');
            $('.community_slides_video > div').attr('aria-label', 'community video of Emerson Estates');
            $('.gatherings_additionalvideo').find('.video-playbutton').removeAttr('aria-hidden');
            //$('.gatherings_additionalvideo').find('.video-playbutton').attr('aria-label','low maintenance living at Emerson Estates');
